[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 31.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"447a9e","wall_time_seconds":10.0010735,"session_id":94522,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"1c8744","wall_time_seconds":10.0192533,"session_id":6213,"original_token_count":1126,"output":"import { type ReactNode } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\\r\\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { Lock } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport { Button } from \'@/components/ui/button\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \'@/hooks/useUserPermissions\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { bootcampIdFromRoute, classroomItemMatchesBootcamp } from \'@/lib/bootcamps\';\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Props {\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The route prefix for this bootcamp, e.g. \\"/bootcamp/principle-flaw\\" or\\r\\n   * \\"/topics/abstract\\". Matched against either `metadata_json.bootcamp_id`\\r\\n   * (slug, see `bootcampIdOverride`) or `source_id` on a classroom_items\\r\\n   * row of type bootcamp_module.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  routePath: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Override the slug extracted from `routePath`. Default behavior strips a\\r\\n   * leading \\"/bootcamp/\\" and trailing path segments, which works for the\\r\\n   * /bootcamp/* family. New surfaces under /topics/* should pass this\\r\\n   * explicitly (e.g. routePath \\"/topics/abstract\\", bootcampIdOverride\\r\\n   * \\"abstraction\\") so classroom_items rows assigned with the legacy slug\\r\\n   * still resolve to the same gate.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  bootcampIdOverride?: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Additional route paths to accept as `source_id` matches. Used when a\\r\\n   * topic has moved (legacy /bootcamp/abstraction → /topics/abstract). The\\r\\n   * current `routePath` is always matched.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  legacyRoutePaths?: string[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The student-facing display name, used only by the locked state.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  title?: string;\\r\\n  children: ReactNode;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-student bootcamp gate. Admins always pass. Students need a\\r\\n * matching `classroom_items` row of type=\'bootcamp_module\' assigned to\\r\\n * them. If no row exists, render a calm \\"Not assigned yet\\" surface.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Direct URL hits go through this gate, so a student typing\\r\\n * /bootcamp/principle-flaw won\'t see anything they weren\'t assigned.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function BootcampGate({\\r\\n  routePath,\\r\\n  bootcampIdOverride,\\r\\n  legacyRoutePaths,\\r\\n  title,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: Props) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const { is_admin, loading: permsLoading } = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const bootcampId =\\r\\n    bootcampIdOverride ??\\r\\n    bootcampIdFromRoute(routePath);\\r\\n  const explicitRoutePaths = [routePath, ...(legacyRoutePaths ?? [])];\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'bootcamp-gate\', user?.id, routePath, bootcampId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user && !is_admin && !permsLoading,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'classroom_items\')\\r\\n        .select(\'id, status, source_id, metadata_json\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'type\', \'bootcamp_module\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'student_id\', user!.id)\\r\\n        .neq(\'status\', \'archived\');\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      const rows = (data ?? []) as Array<{\\r\\n        source_id: string | null;\\r\\n        metadata_json: Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n      }>;\\r\\n      return rows.some(\\r\\n        (r) =>\\r\\n          classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(r, bootcampId) ||\\r\\n          (r.source_id\\r\\n            ? explicitRoutePaths.some((path) => r.source_id === path || r.source_id?.startsWith(`${path}/`))\\r\\n            : false),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!authReady || permsLoading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-zinc-950\\" />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (is_admin || assigned) {\\r\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (isLoading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-zinc-950\\" />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 bg-background\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"max-w-md text-center space-y-4\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mx-auto w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n          <Lock className=\\"w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground\\" strokeWidth={1.75} />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-1.5\\">\\r\\n          <h2 className=\\"text-lg font-semibold\\">Not assigned yet</h2>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed\\">\\r\\n            {title ? `${title} ` : \'This bootcamp \'}\\r\\n            isn\'t part of your current path. Joshua can add it from the Teacher Workspace.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <Button variant=\\"outline\\" size=\\"sm\\" onClick={() => navigate(\'/classroom\')}>\\r\\n          Back to Classroom\\r\\n        </Button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"501ac8","wall_time_seconds":10.0015437,"session_id":57159,"original_token_count":197,"output":"src/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/IntroPathLearningLayout.tsx:4:interface IntroPathLearningLayoutProps {\\nsrc/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/IntroPathLearningLayout.tsx:8:export function IntroPathLearningLayout({ children }: IntroPathLearningLayoutProps) {\\nsrc/bootcamps/main-conclusion/components/IntroPathLearningLayout.tsx:9:  return (\\nsrc/bootcamps/main-conclusion/modules/lessons/notes/IntroLessonPage.tsx:29:export type IntroLessonPageId =\\nsrc/bootcamps/main-conclusion/modules/lessons/notes/IntroLessonPage.tsx:208:  return (\\nsrc/bootcamps/main-conclusion/modules/lessons/notes/IntroLessonPage.tsx:949:export function IntroLessonPage({ pageId }: { pageId: IntroLessonPageId }) {\\nsrc/bootcamps/main-conclusion/modules/lessons/notes/IntroLessonPage.tsx:952:  return (\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Verified architecture:

- `main-conclusion/routes.tsx` mounts all Intro routes under `WorkspaceShell`. `App.tsx` is only an old entry adapter.
- `ModuleIndex.tsx` presents one next-step `RunnerHero`, computed from acknowledged module progress. It does not show the curriculum map.
- Current notes render through `modules/lessons/notes/IntroLessonPage.tsx` → `IntroNotesShell.tsx`. Each note contains authored sections and skill-specific inline interactions.
- `IntroNotesShell` supplies the large note opening, `DynamicIslandTOC`, reading layout, and previous/next action footer.
- `content/introToLrPath.ts` owns the integrated note/tool/drill/practice sequence and completion IDs.
- `IntroGuidedWorkbook.tsx` / `introGuidedSequence.ts` are another workbook flow, not the primary modern note routes.
- `IntroPathLearningLayout` includes `IntegratedPathRail`. Do not reuse it for this request, because it explicitly introduces the path map the user rejected.

Important access distinction: `BootcampGate.tsx` checks whether any active `bootcamp_module` assignment matches the bootcamp. It does **not** limit the visible Intro lessons to currently assigned modules. The assignment-scoped flow is an additional requirement.

Recommended reuse:

1. Extract a generic teaching-note shell from `IntroNotesShell`, passing current/previous/next action objects and a completion callback. Its current helpers and persistence are hardcoded to Intro.
2. Render conditional notes through that shared shell, with relevant original conditional content and inline guided interactions.
3. Keep the conditional save hook and stable question/unit IDs.
4. Replace conditional landing map with the active note or one next-action entry.
5. Define an assignment-scoped action list from actual `classroom_items` targeting. Derive note → guided interaction → drill → next note only within that scope.
6. Reuse Intro’s typography, TOC, section presentation, footer, and minimal workspace chrome. Its `WorkspaceShell`, `LeftRail`, drawer, palette, and tutor contain Intro-specific routes/data, so direct mounting would send students into Intro materials.

Current Conditional deviations: persistent “Teaching sequence” rail, all-unit curriculum cards, repeated separate-page lesson/guided/practice template, and no active-assignment scope enforced beyond query parameters. No edits made during this inspection.

unknown): ConditionalAssignmentTarget | null {\\r\\n  if (!value || typeof value !== \'object\' || Array.isArray(value)) return null;\\r\\n  const target = value as ConditionalAssignmentTarget;\\r\\n  if (![\'course\', \'stage\', \'area\', \'skill\', \'general\'].includes(target.scope) ||\\r\\n    ![\'learn\', \'practice\'].includes(target.mode) || !DIFFICULTIES.includes(target.difficulty)) return null;\\r\\n  if ((target.scope === \'skill\' || target.scope === \'stage\') && !findConditionalUnit(target.unitId)) return null;\\r\\n  if (target.scope === \'area\' && !CONDITIONAL_AREAS.some(area => area.id === target.area)) return null;\\r\\n  return target;\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction legacyTarget(source: string | null): ConditionalAssignmentTarget | null {\\r\\n  if (!source || !source.startsWith(\'/bootcamp/conditionals\')) return null;\\r\\n  const url = new URL(source, \'https://local.invalid\');\\r\\n  const base = \'/bootcamp/conditionals\';\\r\\n  if (url.pathname !== base && !url.pathname.startsWith(`${base}/`)) return null;\\r\\n  const level = url.searchParams.get(\'level\') ?? \'beginner\';\\r\\n  if (!DIFFICULTIES.includes(level as ConditionalDifficulty)) return null;\\r\\n  const difficulty = level as ConditionalDifficulty;\\r\\n  const start = url.searchParams.get(\'start\');\\r\\n  if (start) return parseTarget({ scope: \'stage\', mode: \'learn\', difficulty, unitId: start });\\r\\n  const pathUnit = url.pathname.match(/^\\\\/bootcamp\\\\/conditionals\\\\/(?:lessons|activities)\\\\/([^/]+)$/)?.[1];\\r\\n  const unitId = pathUnit ?? url.searchParams.get(\'unit\');\\r\\n  const area = url.searchParams.get(\'area\');\\r\\n  const mode = url.pathname === `${base}/practice` ? \'practice\' : \'learn\';\\r\\n  if (unitId) return parseTarget({ scope: \'skill\', mode, difficulty, unitId });\\r\\n  if (area) return parseTarget({ scope: \'area\', mode, difficulty, area });\\r\\n  if (url.pathname === `${base}/practice`) return parseTarget({ scope: \'general\', mode: \'practice\', difficulty });\\r\\n  return url.pathname === base && !url.search ? { ...DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET } : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport function conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget): string[] {\\r\\n  if (target.scope === \'course\') return getCurriculumUnits(conditionalUnits).map(unit => unit.id);\\r\\n  if (target.scope === \'stage\') return getCurriculumUnits(conditionalUnits, target.unitId).map(unit => unit.id);\\r\\n  return conditionalUnits.filter(unit => target.scope === \'skill\' ? unit.id === target.unitId\\r\\n    : target.scope === \'area\' ? unit.area === target.area : unit.phase === \'conditional\').map(unit => unit.id);\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport async function resolveConditionalAssignments(rows: ConditionalAssignmentRow[], userId: string, now = Date.now()): Promise<ResolvedConditionalAssignment[]> {\\r\\n  const result: ResolvedConditionalAssignment[] = [];\\r\\n  for (const row of rows) {\\r\\n    if (row.student_id !== userId || row.type !== \'bootcamp_module\' || !row.visible_to_student ||\\r\\n      ![\'assigned\', \'in_progress\', \'completed\'].includes(row.status) || !classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(row, \'conditionals\')) continue;\\r\\n    if (row.unlock_at && (!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(row.unlock_at)) || Date.parse(row.unlock_at) > now)) continue;\\r\\n    const metadata = row.metadata_json;\\r\\n    const target = metadata && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(metadata, resolveConditionalAssignment(target);\\r\\n    result.push({ id: row.id, title: row.title || resolved.title, route: resolved.route, target: resolved.target,\\r\\n      unitIds: conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(resolved.target), mode: resolved.target.mode,\\r\\n      ...(target.scope !== \'course\' && target.scope !== \'stage\' ? { difficulty: resolved.target.difficulty } : {}) });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return result;\\r\\n}\\r\\n/** URL parameters never grant work. Call this against a target read from the student\'s assignment row. */\\r\\nexport function conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(assignment: ResolvedConditionalAssignment, request: {\\r\\n  unitId: string; action: \'lesson\' | \'guided\' | \'practice\'; difficulty?: ConditionalDifficulty;\\r\\n}): boolean {\\r\\n  if (!assignment.unitIds.includes(request.unitId)) return false;\\r\\n  if (request.action !== \'practice\') return assignment.mode === \'learn\';\\r\\n  return request.difficulty === (assignment.difficulty ?? getCurriculumDifficulty(request.unitId));\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport async function conditionalTeacherPreview(): Promise<ResolvedConditionalAssignment> {\\r\\n  const resolved = await resolveConditionalAssignment();\\r\\n  return { id: \'teacher-preview\', title: resolved.title, route: resolved.route, target: resolved.target,\\r\\n    unitIds: conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(resolved.target), mode: \'learn\' };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

useConditionalProgress: () => ({ progress: state.progress, savedProgress: state.saved, status: state.status, error: '', retry: vi.fn(), update: vi.fn() }) }));\nimport { ConditionalBootcampRoutes } from './routes';\nconst base = '/bootcamp/conditionals';\nconst course: ResolvedConditionalAssignment = { id: 'course', title: 'Conditional Logic', route: base, target: { scope: 'course', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'beginner' }, unitIds: conditionalUnits.map(unit => unit.id), mode: 'learn' };\nfunction skill(mode: 'learn' | 'practice' = 'learn'): ResolvedConditionalAssignment {\n  return { id: 'skill', title: 'Group 2', route: base, target: { scope: 'skill', mode, unitId: 'group-2', difficulty: 'advanced' }, unitIds: ['group-2'], mode, difficulty: 'advanced' };\n}\nfunction mount(path: element={<ConditionalBootcampRoutes userId=\"student\" assignments={assignments} isAdmin={isAdmin} />} /></Routes></MemoryRouter>);\n}\nfunction check(questionId: string, unitId: string, difficulty: ConditionalAttempt['difficulty'] = 'beginner'): ConditionalAttempt {\n  return { id: `run:${questionId}`, questionId, unitId, difficulty, choice: 0, correct: false, hint: true, explanationBeforeCheck: false, working: 'My reasoning', checkedAt: '2026-10-01T12:00:00.000Z' };\n}\nfunction reviewed(unitId: string) {\n  const unit = conditionalUnits.find(item => item.id === unitId)!;\n  state.progress!.lessons[unit.id] = '2026-10-01T12:00:00.000Z';\n  state.progress!.attempts.push(check(unit.guidedExample.id, unit.id));\n  return unit;\n}\nfunction finishPractice(unitId: string, difficulty: ConditionalAttempt['difficulty']) {\n  const unit = conditionalUnits.find(item => item.id === unitId)!;\n  state.progress!.attempts.push(...unit.drills[difficulty].map(question => expect(document.querySelector('.cb-sequence')).toBeNull();\n  expect(screen.queryAllByRole('combobox')).toHaveLength(0);\n});\nit('shows only actual assignments and ignores forged broader query })).toBeTruthy();\n});\nit('does not grant lessons to practice-only work', () => {\n  mount(`${base}/lessons/group-2`, [skill('practice')]);\n  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'This activity is not available here.' })).toBeTruthy();\n});\nit('rejects a forged practice level', () => {\n  mount(`${base}/practice?unit=group-2&level=beginner`, [skill()]);\n  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'This activity is not available here.' })).toBeTruthy();\n});\nit('ends targeted work after its own drill instead of offering an unassigned lesson', () => ## assistant

Implemented routes, tests, and styling only.

- Default entry shows one current task, no curriculum map or Teaching Sequence rail.
- Notes use `ConditionalLessonNote`, then guided work and scoped practice.
- Actual assignments govern unit, mode, and level. Forged URL parameters do not grant work.
- Targeted skill assignments end after their drill.
- Reference and review stay within the active assignment.
- Legacy direct links resolve against real grants; unknown explicit assignment IDs fail closed.
- Multiple assignments show only the student’s own entries.
- Admin preview uses the same single-task experience.

16 route tests passed. Targeted ESLint clean before the final two regression additions. No commit. Parent handles browser checks, build, and publication.
